class="TextFragment-styles__TextFragmentStyled-sc-99e7c69b-0 jlzOpL">Many of the people on the FBI&#x27;s cyber most wanted list are Russian. While some allegedly work for the government earning a normal salary, others are accused of making a fortune from ransomware attacks and online theft. If they left Russia they&#x27;d be arrested - but at home they appear to be given free rein.</b></p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">&quot;We&#x27;re wasting our time,&quot; I thought, as I watched a cat licking the carcass of a discarded takeaway chicken.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">Surely there would no longer be any trace of an alleged multi-millionaire cyber-criminal on this dilapidated estate in a run-down town 700km (400 miles) east of Moscow.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">But I pressed on with an interpreter and cameraman, shooing the mangy cat away from the entrance to the block of flats.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">When we knocked at one of the doors, a young man answered and a curious elderly woman peered around the corner at us from the kitchen.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">&quot;Igor Turashev? No, I don&#x27;t recognise the name,&quot; he said. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">&quot;His family is registered here, so who are you?&quot; we asked.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">After some friendly chat we explained we were reporters from the BBC, and the mood suddenly changed.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">&quot;I&#x27;m not telling you where he is and you shouldn&#x27;t try to find him. You shouldn&#x27;t have come here,&quot; the young man said angrily.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">I didn&#x27;t sleep well that night, thinking of the conflicting advice I&#x27;d been given by people in the security sector.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">Some said trying to track down wanted cyber-criminals on their home soil was risky. &quot;They&#x27;ll have armed guards,&quot; I was told. &quot;You&#x27;ll end up in a ditch somewhere,&quot; another warned. Others said it would be fine - &quot;They&#x27;re just computer geeks.&quot;</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 fqhmpl">All said we wouldn&#x27;t get anywhere near them.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="image-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 kugUwP"><figure class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 tlbDj"><figcaption class="Caption-styles__CaptionStyled-sc-70433723-0 fLsOPx">Igor Turashev is accused of being a system administrator for Evil Corp</figcaption></div></figure></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">Nor is Igor Turashev, 40, keeping a low profile.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">Using public records, my colleague Andrey Zakharov, BBC Russia&#x27;s Cyber Reporter, found three companies registered in his name. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">All have offices in Moscow&#x27;s prestigious Federation Tower, a shiny skyscraper in the financial district that wouldn&#x27;t look out of place in Manhattan or London&#x27;s Canary Wharf. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">A puzzled receptionist looked for a phone number, and found that the offices didn&#x27;t have one. She did find a mobile phone under the firm&#x27;s name though, and put us through. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">We called it and waited. A Frank Sinatra song played for about five minutes, then finally someone picked up, sounding as though he was on a busy street - only to hang up when we said we were journalists. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">As Andrey explained, Turashev is not wanted in Russia so no-one is stopping him renting this expensive city-centre office space.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">It may also be convenient for him to be located among financial companies, including some that deal in the cryptocurrencies, such as Bitcoin, that Evil Corp is alleged to have collected from victims in ransomware attacks - reportedly $10m-worth in one case.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl"><a target="_blank" href="https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2021-11-03/bitcoin-money-laundering-happening-in-moscow-s-vostok-tower-experts-say" class="LinkUrl-styles__LinkUrlStyled-sc-f4e3b82f-0 eGAhOs">A Bloomberg report</a> using research from Bitcoin analysts Chainalysis claims that the Federation Tower houses numerous crypto firms that act like &quot;cash machines for cyber-criminals&quot;.  </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">We tried two other addresses linked to Turashev and another key Evil Corp figure called Denis Gusev, and made numerous approaches by phone and email, but no-one answered.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">Andrey and I spent a long time trying to find a place of work for Maksim Yakubets. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">He used to be a director of his mother&#x27;s cattle feed company, but these days he appears to have no registered business or employer.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">What we did find, though, were addresses where he might still live, so one night we went to give them a knock.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">At one, a man laughed over the intercom as we explained where we were from. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">&quot;Maksim Yakubets isn&#x27;t here. He hasn&#x27;t been here for probably 15 years. I&#x27;m his dad,&quot; he said. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">To our surprise Yakubets senior then came out into the hallway and gave us an impassioned 20-minute interview on camera, angrily condemning the US authorities for indicting his son.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="video-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 kugUwP"><figure class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 ieGDnl"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 enRivQ"><div class="Player-styles__PlayerStyled-sc-6416b0a0-1 duMWSj"><div data-testid="" class="SpaPlayerInner-styles__SpaPlayerInnerStyled-sc-82491dc0-0 hlPmIm"></div><button data-testid="player-play-button" aria-label="Play video" tabindex="0" class="CustomCTA-styles__CtaContainerButtonStyled-sc-be08601b-1 fZevdn"><div data-testid="cta-play-icon" class="CustomCTA-styles__CtaPlayIconStyled-sc-be08601b-0 itUMiZ"><svg viewBox="0 0 82 82"><circle cx="41" cy="41" r="37" fill="#fff"></circle><polygon fill="#202224" points="32,25 32,58 60,42"></polygon></svg></div><div class="CustomCTA-styles__CtaInfoStyled-sc-be08601b-4 lmHaCb"></div></button></div><figcaption class="Caption-styles__CaptionStyled-sc-70433723-0 fLsOPx">Maksim Yakubets doesn&#x27;t answer calls and emails, so Joe Tidy knocks on a door where he once lived - and speaks to his father</figcaption></div></figure></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">The $5m US reward for information leading to his son&#x27;s arrest - the highest ever bounty for a named cyber-criminal - had led the family to live in fear of attack, Mr Yakubets said, demanding that we publish his words.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">&quot;The Americans created a problem for my family, for many people who know us, for our relatives. What was the purpose? American justice has turned into Soviet justice. He was not questioned, he was not interrogated, there were no procedures that would prove his guilt.&quot;</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">He denied that his son was a cyber-criminal. When I asked how he thought he had become so rich, he laughed, saying that I was exaggerating the price tag of the wedding and that the luxury cars were rented. Maksim&#x27;s salary was higher than average, he said, because &quot;he works, he gets paid, he has a job&quot;.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">&quot;What does he do for work then?&quot; I asked.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">&quot;Why should I tell you?&quot; he replied. &quot;What about our private lives?&quot;</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">He said he hadn&#x27;t had any contact with his son since the indictment, so could not put us in touch with him.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="image-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 kugUwP"><figure class="Image-styles__ImageStyled-sc-8c99a12b-0 cVsHni"/></div></div></figure></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">Yakubets and Turashev are part of the growing list of Russian citizens to be issued with cyber-sanctions as the West struggles to respond to cyber-attacks.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">More Russian people and organisations have been sanctioned and indicted than those of any other nationality.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">Indictments prevent the hackers from travelling abroad, while the sanctions freeze any assets they have in the West, and ban them from doing business with Western firms.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">Last year the European Union started issuing cyber-sanctions, following in the US&#x27;s footsteps, and it&#x27;s mainly Russians who have been named and shamed on this list too.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">The vast majority of the individuals on these lists are said to have direct links to the Russian state, hacking in order to spy, project power or exert pressure. While all nations hack each other, the US, EU and allies claim that some of the Russian attacks cross a line, in terms of what is acceptable. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">Some of the men are accused of <a target="_self" href="https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/technology-38573074" class="LinkUrl-styles__LinkUrlStyled-sc-f4e3b82f-0 eGAhOs">causing widespread blackouts in Ukraine</a> by hacking power grids. Others are wanted for trying to <a target="_self" href="https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/world-europe-45747472" class="LinkUrl-styles__LinkUrlStyled-sc-f4e3b82f-0 eGAhOs">hack into a chemical weapons testing facility</a> in the wake of the Salisbury poisonings.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">The Kremlin denies all accusations, routinely laughing them off as Western hysteria and &quot;Russophobia&quot;.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">As there are no clear rules for what is acceptable nation state hacking, we deliberately concentrated our investigation on the individuals accused of being criminals, hacking for profit. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="image-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 kugUwP"><figure class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 ieGDnl"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 tlbDj"><div data-testid="image" class="Image-styles__ImageCardStyled-sc-8c99a12b-1 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">So do cyber-sanctions against &quot;criminal&quot; hackers work?</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">Speaking to Yakubets&#x27; father it seems that they do have some impact - at the very least they made him furious. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">However Evil Corp appears to have been unaffected. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div researchers allege the crew are still carrying out lucrative cyber-attacks on mainly Western targets.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">The &quot;golden rule&quot; of Russian hacking, according to researchers and former hackers, is that non-state-employed criminal hackers can hack who they like, as long as the victims are not in Russian-speaking or former Soviet territories.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">The rule appears to work, as cyber-security researchers have for many years noticed fewer attacks in those countries. They&#x27;ve also found that some malware is designed to avoid computers with Russian language systems.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">Lilia Yapparova, an investigative reporter working at Meduza, one of few independent news organisations in the country, says the golden rule is helpful for the intelligence services, which can then exploit the skills hackers have developed while working for themselves.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">&quot;It&#x27;s more valuable for the FSB to enlist hackers in Russia than to put them in jail. One of my sources, who is an ex-FSB officer, told me that he personally tried to enlist some of the guys from Evil Corp to do some work for him,&quot; she says. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">The US claims that Maksim Yakubets and other wanted hackers - including Evgeniy Bogachev, who has a $3m bounty out for his arrest - have worked directly for the intelligence services.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">It may not be a coincidence that Yakubets&#x27; father-in-law, seen in the wedding video, is a former high-level member of the FSB.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">We asked the Russian government to comment on the fact that hackers seem to operate freely in Russia, but received no reply.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">When Vladimir Putin was asked about this at the Geneva summit with Joe Biden this summer, he <a target="_self" href="https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/technology-57504007" class="LinkUrl-styles__LinkUrlStyled-sc-f4e3b82f-0 eGAhOs">denied that high-profile attacks were originating in his country</a> and even claimed that most cyber-attacks began in the US. But he said he would work with the US to &quot;bring order&quot;. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="image-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 kugUwP"><figure class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 ieGDnl"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 tlbDj"><div data-testid="image" hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 tlbDj"><h2 class="Headline-styles__HeadlineStyled-sc-6b3bb442-0 knYLIy">The rise of Evil Corp </h2></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="unordered-list-list" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 kugUwP"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 tlbDj"><ul class="List-styles__ListStyled-sc-70c6f140-0 gpSChY"><li class="ListItem-styles__ListItemStyled-sc-cb54891-0 eHDLAF">2009: Evil Corp arrives on the scene, allegedly using malware called Cridex, Dridex, Bugat or Zeus to steal banking logins and grab money from accounts</li><li class="ListItem-styles__ListItemStyled-sc-cb54891-0 eHDLAF">2012: Members of Evil Corp are indicted by a court in Nebraska under their online monikers, as their identities are unknown (Yakubets allegedly goes under the name &quot;Aqua&quot;)</li><li class="ListItem-styles__ListItemStyled-sc-cb54891-0 eHDLAF">2017: The crew is accused of starting a &quot;ransomware as a service&quot; (RaaS) operation - it&#x27;s claimed other hackers pay to use their ransomware, called BitPaymer </li><li class="ListItem-styles__ListItemStyled-sc-cb54891-0 eHDLAF">2019: Yakubets, Turashev and seven others are indicted, sanctioned or designated in the US - a $5m bounty is offered for information leading to Yakubets&#x27; arrest</li><li class="ListItem-styles__ListItemStyled-sc-cb54891-0 eHDLAF">Since 2019, Evil Corp is alleged to have cycled through different brands and variants of ransomware including DoppelPaymer, Grief, WastedLocker, Hades, Phoenix and Macaw</li></ul></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="image-block" hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">In the last six months the US and its allies have gone beyond cyber-sanctions, and started employing a far more aggressive tactic. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">They have begun hacking back against cyber-crime gangs and have successfully <a target="_self" href="https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/technology-59215167" class="LinkUrl-styles__LinkUrlStyled-sc-f4e3b82f-0 eGAhOs">taken some of them offline</a>, at least temporarily. REvil and DarkSide have announced on forums that they are no longer operating because of law enforcement action.</p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">On two occasions US government hackers have even managed to retrieve millions of dollars of Bitcoin stolen from victims.  </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">An international effort involving Europol and the US Department of Justice has also seen alleged hackers arrested in South Korea, Kuwait, Romania and Ukraine. </p></div></div></div></div><div><div data-component="text-block" class="Container-styles__ContainerStyled-sc-8b855a6c-0 cPmzSx"><div class="Grid-styles__GridStyled-sc-70d90311-0 hRsXiV"><div class="GridItem-styles__GridItemStyled-sc-6cc20e4a-0 bnCeGm"><p class="Paragraph-styles__ParagraphStyled-sc-ffb5f5ba-0 fqhmpl">However, cyber security researchers say more groups are surfacing, and attacks are occurring every week.
